================================================================================
FILE: README.md
================================================================================
# Silicon Valley Bank and Trust

Complete local reconstruction from SVBT Full Scale Build.pdf and Timothy Powers BI.pdf, with the supplied Tim Power portrait. React 19, Vite, Three.js, Node.js 24, Express 5, native SQLite, scrypt and PDFKit. No external APIs, analytics, remotely loaded fonts or cloud database are used at runtime. Package installation requires internet access unless npm dependencies are already cached.

## Start on Windows

1. Install Node.js 24 or newer from nodejs.org. Close and reopen your terminal after installation.
2. Open PowerShell in this project folder.
3. Run:

```powershell
npm ci
Copy-Item .env.example .env
npm run seed
npm run dev
```

4. Open **http://localhost:3000** in current Chrome, Edge or Firefox. Use exactly `localhost`, not `127.0.0.1`, for the configured browser origin. Allow cookies. Secure cookies are supported on localhost by modern browsers. Do not use a custom plain-HTTP hostname; use HTTPS for custom names.
5. Keep the terminal open. Press Ctrl+C to stop. Database and issued statements persist across restarts.

On macOS/Linux, replace the copy command with `cp .env.example .env`; all npm commands are identical.

First startup also seeds automatically when DEMO_SEED=true. The seed command is idempotent and verifies the four ending balances. It refuses to conceal later ledger corrections by overwriting history.

## Demonstration logins

| Access | Email | Password |
| --- | --- | --- |
| VIP customer | sales@bakhtiariautogroup.com | FounderClient!2026 |
| Moderator | tpower@siliconvalleybankandtrust.com | ModeratorGod!2026 |

Customer: use Log in. Moderator: use Moderator access in the footer. Both screens authenticate against the same server; the screen does not determine permissions. These passwords are public local-demo credentials only.

Before the FIRST seed, change DEMO_CUSTOMER_EMAIL, DEMO_CUSTOMER_PASSWORD, DEMO_MODERATOR_EMAIL and DEMO_MODERATOR_PASSWORD in .env to customize demo credentials. Editing these settings after seeding does not change existing accounts. Use a new DB_PATH for a new demonstration instead of erasing immutable records.

## Exact seeded statements

Bakhtiari Auto Group, LLC — 3155 Stevens Creek San Jose CA 95117. The generated account number is a unique 12-digit number, not a real bank account.

| Month | Opening | Deposits | Expenses | Ending |
| --- | ---: | ---: | ---: | ---: |
| June 2026 | $0 | $10,000 | $5,767 | $4,233 |
| July 2026 | $4,233 | $15,000 | $5,884 | $13,349 |
| August 2026 | $13,349 | $15,000 | $6,001 | $22,348 |
| September 2026 | $22,348 | $15,000 | $6,118 | $31,230 |

Each month has a deposit on the second plus 12 deterministic expenses. Every balance derives from integer-cent ledger entries. Four revision-1 PDFs are created in monthly/statements/. The source PDF has a displaced opening-balance table; this reconstruction follows its stated zero-opening and carry-forward rules and exact ending balances.

## Using the workspaces

Customers can view their own balance, transaction-derived chart, search/month-filter history, print history/account details, and update name/address only after confirming the current password. New registrations are customer-only and start at zero.

Moderators select customer accounts, post positive credits or negative debits, and reverse mistakes with an opposite-signed new ledger entry. Original entries cannot be edited or deleted. Investment records can be created/edited with sector, status, target capital, risk and strategy; these records are not public.

In Statements, choose a month, calculate the live preview, then issue a revision. Download its PDF from the archive and run its integrity check. PDFs contain statement dates, account identity, opening/closing amounts, deposits/debits, transaction running balances, revision and page numbers. Statements and statement APIs are moderator-only, as required by the surviving specification.

Backdated transactions change the affected month AND later months' live opening balances. Issue replacement revisions for EVERY affected month. Earlier issued revisions remain immutable; no role can rewrite an issued document, including Tim. This resolves the source's editing language in favor of its immutable-ledger/revision requirement. Audit records capture operations. Integrity checks compare snapshot hashes, stored PDF bytes and filesystem archive bytes; they are not a substitute for independently secured backups.

## Tim Power handover

The About Us / Leadership section uses Tim's supplied portrait and biography. The biography primarily says Timothy Power, while the build document says Timothy Powers; the public source note preserves that discrepancy for his approval. No unsupported employment details, founding date, licensing or FDIC claims were invented.

Tim will complete and approve banking licensing, FDIC and other regulatory information before public launch. This build deliberately identifies itself as a demonstration until those facts and operational approvals are verified. The requested www.siliconvalleybankandtrust.com domain has not been connected or modified.

To provision a clean moderator without demonstration customers, stop the server and set DEMO_SEED=false and DB_PATH=data/handover.sqlite in .env. Then in PowerShell:

```powershell
$env:MODERATOR_EMAIL = "tpower@siliconvalleybankandtrust.com"
$secret = Read-Host "Enter a unique moderator password (12 or more characters)" -AsSecureString
$env:MODERATOR_PASSWORD = [System.Net.NetworkCredential]::new("", $secret).Password
npm run create-moderator
Remove-Item Env:MODERATOR_PASSWORD
npm run dev
```

The administrative script does not promote public registrations and rejects demo passwords and existing email addresses. For password rotation of an existing moderator, stop the application and run the same script with MODERATOR_RESET=true and a unique new password; existing sessions are revoked. Keep .env and production credentials out of version control, archives and email. Deliver real credentials through a separate secure channel.

## Build and verify

```powershell
npm run lint
npm test
npm run test:ui
npm run build
npm start
```

npm run test:ui uses installed Microsoft Edge to verify the public page, WebGL canvas, mobile sector dialog, portrait loading, customer login/logout, exact balance, moderator preview/integrity and PDF download. It starts its own local server on port 3005; that port must be free. On machines without Edge, install it or change channel in playwright.config.js and install the matching Playwright browser. The application itself does not require Edge.

npm start serves dist and the protected APIs from one origin. With development settings it is still a local demonstration. Tests use independent temporary databases and HTTP servers; they verify exact monthly continuity, PDF text, server roles, CSRF/origin checks, hashed sessions/passwords, session rotation/expiry, customer isolation, unique accounts, immutability, reversals, investments, audit records, concurrency and moderator provisioning.

## Production constraints

This is a single-instance local banking prototype, NOT a certified live bank platform. Completing licensing/FDIC text alone does not make it suitable to hold real funds. Live launch also requires bank-approved core ledger/database, compliance review, identity verification, MFA, hardened hosting, backups/disaster recovery, monitoring, penetration testing and applicable legal approvals.

Use NODE_ENV=production, DEMO_SEED=false, a fresh DB_PATH, and explicit HTTPS APP_ORIGIN. Seeded/default-password databases must never be deployed. Terminate HTTPS at an approved reverse proxy; TRUST_PROXY=true trusts exactly one proxy and is safe only if direct public access to Node is blocked. Cookies are always Secure, HttpOnly and SameSite=Strict. Server origin and CSRF enforcement remain mandatory. The Node process binds loopback by default. Keep database, statement archives and secrets readable only by authorized operators. Back up SQLite and its WAL consistently using approved SQLite backup tools; coordinate backups with immutable PDF archives.

The original source's suggested HTTP hosts-file domain cannot safely carry its required Secure cookies, so it is not the default installation path. Localhost works without publishing or changing DNS. Public DNS requires the domain owner's authorization and hosting; no DNS is changed by this project.

## Project tree

```text
SVBT/
  .env.example
  .gitignore
  README.md
  SOURCE.txt
  package.json
  package-lock.json
  index.html
  vite.config.js
  eslint.config.js
  playwright.config.js
  public/tim-power.jpg
  src/main.jsx
  src/App.jsx
  src/Portal.jsx
  src/Scene.jsx
  src/styles.css
  server/index.js
  server/db.js
  server/services.js
  server/pdf.js
  server/seed.js
  server/create-moderator.js
  scripts/import-portrait.js
  scripts/package.js
  tests/banking.test.js
  tests/pdf.test.js
  tests/ui/workspace.spec.js
  data/bank.sqlite (created at seed)
  monthly/statements/*.pdf (created at seed)
  dist/ (created at build)
```

SOURCE.txt provides the full contents of every project text file, including the dependency lockfile; the portrait is encoded with a base64 prefix. SVBT-complete.zip contains the complete files, bundled build and demonstration statements. No node_modules, private .env, database, original attachments or temporary tooling are included. Run npm ci after extraction. Source and archive can be regenerated with node scripts/package.js.

## Design verification

The site implements responsive navy/copper/ice styling, real extruded Three.js geometry, orbit controls, pause/reduced-motion handling, offscreen suspension and four procedural sector scenes. Inspect desktop and mobile browser widths and use a WebGL-enabled browser to evaluate the visual presentation. Automated build/API/PDF checks do not certify that the design meets a subjective visual standard.

export function Modal({ title, children, onClose, wide = false }) {
  const ref = useRef(null);
  const titleId = useId();
  useEffect(() => {
    const prior = document.activeElement;
    const overflow = document.body.style.overflow;
    document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
    ref.current.showModal();
    return () => { document.body.style.overflow = overflow; prior?.focus(); };
  }, []);
  return <dialog ref={ref} aria-labelledby={titleId} className={wide ? 'modal wide' : 'modal'} onCancel={e => { e.preventDefault(); onClose(); }} onClick={e => { if (e.target === ref.current) onClose(); }}><div className="modal-head"><span className="eyebrow">SVBT / LOCAL DEMONSTRATION</span><button className="icon-button" onClick={onClose} aria-label="Close dialog">×</button></div><h2 id={titleId}>{title}</h2>{children}</dialog>;
}

export function SceneView({ kind = 'brand', label }) {
  const ref = useRef(null);
  const [ready, setReady] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => { if (entries.some(e => e.isIntersecting)) { setReady(true); observer.disconnect(); } }, { rootMargin: '160px' });
    observer.observe(ref.current);
    return () => observer.disconnect();
  }, []);
  return <div ref={ref} className={`scene-wrap ${kind === 'brand' ? 'brand-scene' : ''}`}><Suspense fallback={<div className="scene-fallback">SVBT<span>Loading spatial experience</span></div>}>{ready ? <Scene kind={kind} label={label || 'Interactive extruded SVBT monogram'} /> : <div className="scene-fallback">SVBT<span>Spatial perspectives</span></div>}</Suspense></div>;
}

  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const token = useRef('');
  const api = useCallback(async (path, options = {}) => {
    const response = await fetch(`/api${path}`, { credentials: 'same-origin', ...options, headers: { ...(options.body ? { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } : {}), ...(options.method && options.method !== 'GET' ? { 'X-CSRF-Token': token.current } : {}), ...options.headers } });
    const data = await response.json().catch(() => ({}));
    if (data.csrfToken) token.current = data.csrfToken;
    if (!response.ok) {
      if (response.status === 401 && path !== '/login' && path !== '/profile') { setSession({ user: null }); setWorkspace(false); }
      throw new Error(data.error || `Request failed (${response.status})`);
    }
    return data;
  }, []);
  const loadSession = useCallback(async () => {
    try { setSessionError(''); setSession(await api('/session')); } catch { setSessionError('The local server is unavailable. Start the application server, then retry.'); }
  }, [api]);

  async function authenticate(e) {

    const values = Object.fromEntries(new FormData(e.currentTarget));

    try { const result = await api(dialog === 'register' ? '/register' : '/login', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(values) }); setSession(result); setDialog(null); setWorkspace(true); window.scrollTo(0, 0); } catch (err) { setError(err.message); } finally { setBusy(false); }
  }

import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { Modal } from './App.jsx';

const money = value => new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', { style: 'currency', currency: 'USD' }).format((value || 0) / 100);
const today = () => { const date = new Date(); return `${date.getFullYear()}-${String(date.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0')}-${String(date.getDate()).padStart(2, '0')}`; };
function cents(value) {
  if (!/^-?\d+(\.\d{1,2})?$/.test(String(value))) throw new Error('Enter a USD amount with no more than two decimal places.');
  const result = Math.round(Number(value) * 100);
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(result)) throw new Error('Amount is outside the supported range.');
  return result;
}
function Field({ label, ...props }) { return <label>{label}<input {...props} /></label>; }
function Transactions({ rows, reverse, all = rows, running = false }) {
  return <div className="table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>Date</th><th>Description</th><th className="numeric">Amount</th>{running && <th className="numeric">Running balance</th>}{reverse && <th>Correction</th>}</tr></thead><tbody>{rows.length ? rows.map(t => <tr key={t.id}><td className="nowrap">{t.date}</td><td>{t.description}<small className="transaction-id">Entry #{t.id}{t.reversalOf ? ` · Reverses #${t.reversalOf}` : all.some(r => r.reversalOf === t.id) ? ' · Reversed' : ''}</small></td><td className={`numeric ${t.amount > 0 ? 'positive' : ''}`}>{money(t.amount)}</td>{running && <td className="numeric">{money(t.runningBalance)}</td>}{reverse && <td>{!t.reversalOf && !all.some(r => r.reversalOf === t.id) ? <button className="text-button" onClick={() => reverse(t)}>Reverse</button> : <span className="muted">Recorded</span>}</td>}</tr>) : <tr><td colSpan={5} className="empty">No transactions match this view.</td></tr>}</tbody></table></div>;
}
function Chart({ transactions }) {
  const series = useMemo(() => { let balance = 0; return [{ date: 'Opening', balance: 0 }, ...transactions.map(t => ({ date: t.date, balance: balance += t.amount }))]; }, [transactions]);
  const low = Math.min(...series.map(p => p.balance)), high = Math.max(...series.map(p => p.balance));
  const range = high - low || 1;
  const points = series.map((p, i) => `${36 + i / Math.max(series.length - 1, 1) * 828},${180 - (p.balance - low) / range * 145}`).join(' ');
  return <div className="ledger-chart"><div className="chart-labels"><span>{money(high)}</span><span>Running balance · actual ledger entries</span></div><svg viewBox="0 0 900 210" role="img" aria-label={`Ledger running balance across ${transactions.length} transactions. Highest balance ${money(high)}, lowest ${money(low)}.`}><defs><linearGradient id="chartFill" x1="0" y1="0" x2="0" y2="1"><stop offset="0%" stopColor="#9cdeed" stopOpacity=".25" /><stop offset="100%" stopColor="#9cdeed" stopOpacity="0" /></linearGradient></defs>{[35, 83, 131, 180].map(y => <line key={y} x1="36" x2="864" y1={y} y2={y} stroke="#263544" strokeDasharray="4 6" />)}<polygon points={`36,190 ${points} 864,190`} fill="url(#chartFill)" /><polyline points={points} fill="none" stroke="#a5e3f0" strokeWidth="2.5" />{series.map((p, i) => <circle key={i} cx={36 + i / Math.max(series.length - 1, 1) * 828} cy={180 - (p.balance - low) / range * 145} r="3" fill="#c99573"><title>{p.date}: {money(p.balance)}</title></circle>)}</svg><div className="chart-labels"><span>{transactions[0]?.date || 'Zero opening balance'}</span><span>{transactions.at(-1)?.date || 'No activity yet'}</span></div></div>;
}

export default function Portal({ user, api, onLogout, onUser }) {
  const admin = user.role === 'moderator';
  const [tab, setTab] = useState('Overview');
  const [accounts, setAccounts] = useState([]);
  const [accountId, setAccountId] = useState('');
  const [data, setData] = useState(null);
  const [profile, setProfile] = useState(null);
  const [investments, setInvestments] = useState([]);
  const [audit, setAudit] = useState([]);
  const [archive, setArchive] = useState([]);
  const [preview, setPreview] = useState(null);
  const [month, setMonth] = useState(new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 7));
  const [filterMonth, setFilterMonth] = useState('');
  const [search, setSearch] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState('');
  const [notice, setNotice] = useState('');
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false);
  const [edit, setEdit] = useState(null);
  const [integrity, setIntegrity] = useState({});
  const [refresh, setRefresh] = useState(0);
  const base = `/admin/accounts/${accountId}`;
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    Promise.all([api('/profile'), ...(admin ? [api('/admin/accounts')] : [])]).then(([p, list]) => { if (!active) return; setProfile(p); if (list) { setAccounts(list); setAccountId(current => list.some(a => String(a.id) === current) ? current : String(list[0]?.id || '')); } }).catch(e => { if (active) setError(e.message); }).finally(() => { if (active) setLoading(false); });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [admin, refresh, api]);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (admin && !accountId) return;
    let active = true; setLoading(true); setData(null); setPreview(null); setArchive([]); setFilterMonth('');
    api(admin ? base : '/account').then(result => { if (active) { setData(result); if (result.monthly.length) setMonth(result.monthly.at(-1).month); } }).catch(e => { if (active) setError(e.message); }).finally(() => { if (active) setLoading(false); });
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [admin, accountId, refresh, api, base]);
  useEffect(() => {
    let active = true;
    if (!admin) return;
    const requests = tab === 'Investments' ? [api('/admin/investments').then(v => active && setInvestments(v))] : tab === 'Audit' ? [api('/admin/audit').then(v => active && setAudit(v))] : tab === 'Statements' && accountId ? [api(`${base}/statements`).then(v => active && setArchive(v))] : [];
    Promise.all(requests).catch(e => active && setError(e.message));
    return () => { active = false; };
  }, [tab, admin, accountId, refresh, api, base]);
  async function perform(task, message) {
    setBusy(true); setError(''); setNotice('');
    try { await task(); if (message) setNotice(message); return true; } catch (e) { setError(e.message); return false; } finally { setBusy(false); }
  }
  async function saveEntry(e) {
    e.preventDefault(); const form = e.currentTarget; const values = Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form));
    await perform(async () => { if (edit.type === 'reverse') await api(`/admin/transactions/${edit.item.id}/reverse`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(values) }); else { values.amount = cents(values.amount); await api(`${base}/transactions`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(values) }); } setEdit(null); setRefresh(r => r + 1); }, 'Ledger updated. Live previews have changed; existing issued revisions remain unchanged.');
  }
  async function saveInvestment(e) {
    e.preventDefault(); const values = Object.fromEntries(new FormData(e.currentTarget));
    await perform(async () => { values.targetCapital = cents(values.targetCapital); await api(`/admin/investments${edit.item?.id ? `/${edit.item.id}` : ''}`, { method: edit.item?.id ? 'PUT' : 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(values) }); setEdit(null); setRefresh(r => r + 1); }, 'Investment record saved.');
  }
  async function saveProfile(e) {
    e.preventDefault(); const form = e.currentTarget; const values = Object.fromEntries(new FormData(form));
    await perform(async () => { const result = await api('/profile', { method: 'PATCH', body: JSON.stringify(values) }); setProfile(result); onUser({ id: result.id, name: result.name, email: result.email, role: result.role }); form.elements.password.value = ''; setRefresh(r => r + 1); }, 'Personal details updated after password verification.');
  }
  async function pdf(statement) {
    await perform(async () => { const response = await fetch(`/api/admin/statements/${statement.id}/pdf`, { credentials: 'same-origin' }); if (!response.ok) { const result = await response.json().catch(() => ({})); throw new Error(result.error || 'Unable to download statement.'); } const url = URL.createObjectURL(await response.blob()); const link = document.createElement('a'); link.href = url; link.download = `business-checking-${statement.month}-r${statement.revision}.pdf`; link.click(); setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(url), 10000); }, 'Issued PDF downloaded.');
  }
  const rows = (data?.transactions || []).filter(t => (!filterMonth || t.date.startsWith(filterMonth)) && `${t.description} ${t.id} ${t.date} ${money(t.amount)}`.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())).slice().reverse();
  const tabs = admin ? ['Overview', 'Transactions', 'Statements', 'Investments', 'Audit', 'Profile'] : ['Overview', 'Transactions', 'Account details', 'Profile'];
  return <div className="workspace"><aside className="workspace-sidebar"><span className="eyebrow">{admin ? 'OPERATIONS CONSOLE' : 'BUSINESS WORKSPACE'}</span><h3>{user.name}</h3><span className="role-badge">Server-verified {user.role}</span><nav aria-label="Workspace navigation">{tabs.map(t => <button key={t} className={tab === t ? 'active' : ''} aria-current={tab === t ? 'page' : undefined} onClick={() => { setTab(t); setError(''); setNotice(''); }}>{t}<span>↗</span></button>)}</nav><div className="sidebar-bottom"><p>Demonstration funds only.<br />No real banking operations.</p><button className="text-button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => perform(onLogout)}>Log out ↗</button></div></aside><div className="workspace-main"><div className="workspace-heading"><div><span className="eyebrow">SVBT / {admin ? 'MODERATOR' : 'CUSTOMER'}</span><h1>{tab === 'Overview' ? 'Your financial perspective.' : tab}</h1></div><button className="text-button" disabled={busy || loading} onClick={() => { setRefresh(r => r + 1); }}>Refresh ↻</button></div>{admin && <label className="account-select">Selected customer account<select value={accountId} onChange={e => { setAccountId(e.target.value); setError(''); setNotice(''); }} disabled={busy}><option value="" disabled>Select an account</option>{accounts.map(a => <option key={a.id} value={a.id}>{a.name} · {a.number}</option>)}</select></label>}{error && <div className="error" role="alert">{error}<button className="text-button" onClick={() => setRefresh(r => r + 1)}>Retry data</button></div>}{notice && <div className="success" role="status">{notice}</div>}{loading && <p role="status">Loading verified account data…</p>}
    {tab === 'Overview' && data && <><div className="balance-grid"><div className="panel balance-card"><span className="eyebrow">BUSINESS CHECKING / DEMO USD</span><h2>{money(data.balance)}</h2><p>{data.account.name}</p><span className="muted">Account {data.account.number}</span></div><div className="panel"><span className="eyebrow">LEDGER ACTIVITY</span><h2>{data.transactions.length}</h2><p>Recorded transactions</p><span className="muted">Opening balance $0.00 · balance derived from ledger</span></div></div><section className="panel"><div className="panel-heading"><h3>Balance over time</h3><span className="eyebrow">ALL RECORDED ACTIVITY</span></div><Chart transactions={data.transactions} /></section><section className="panel"><div className="panel-heading"><h3>Recent activity</h3><button className="text-button" onClick={() => setTab('Transactions')}>View all ↗</button></div><Transactions rows={data.transactions.slice(-5).reverse()} all={data.transactions} /></section></>}
    {tab === 'Transactions' && data && <><div className="toolbar"><label>Search ledger<input type="search" value={search} onChange={e => setSearch(e.target.value)} placeholder="Description, entry, date or amount" /></label><label>Month<select value={filterMonth} onChange={e => setFilterMonth(e.target.value)}><option value="">All months</option>{data.monthly.map(m => <option key={m.month}>{m.month}</option>)}</select></label>{admin ? <button className="button small" disabled={busy} onClick={() => setEdit({ type: 'post' })}>Post transaction +</button> : <button className="text-button" onClick={() => window.print()}>Print history ↗</button>}</div><section className="panel"><div className="panel-heading"><h3>Immutable transaction ledger</h3><span>{rows.length} entries</span></div><p className="fine-print">{admin ? 'Corrections create an opposite signed entry. Original entries cannot be edited or deleted.' : 'Read-only account history. Your balance reflects all entries, including recorded corrections.'}</p><Transactions rows={rows} all={data.transactions} reverse={admin ? t => setEdit({ type: 'reverse', item: t }) : null} /></section><section className="panel"><h3>Monthly history</h3><div className="table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>Month</th><th>Opening</th><th>Deposits</th><th>Debits</th><th>Closing</th></tr></thead><tbody>{data.monthly.map(m => <tr key={m.month}><td><button className="text-button" onClick={() => setFilterMonth(m.month)}>{m.month}</button></td><td>{money(m.opening)}</td><td>{money(m.deposits)}</td><td>{money(m.debits)}</td><td>{money(m.closing)}</td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>{!data.monthly.length && <p>No monthly activity yet.</p>}</section></>}
    {tab === 'Account details' && data && <section className="panel"><h3>Business checking · local demo</h3><dl><dt>Account holder</dt><dd>{data.account.name}</dd><dt>Account number</dt><dd>{data.account.number}</dd><dt>Address</dt><dd>{data.account.address}</dd><dt>Currency</dt><dd>USD · integer cents ledger</dd><dt>Balance</dt><dd>{money(data.balance)}</dd></dl><button className="text-button" onClick={() => window.print()}>Print account details ↗</button><p className="fine-print">Investment records and issued statements are restricted to moderators.</p></section>}
    {tab === 'Profile' && profile && <section className="panel profile-panel"><h3>Personal details</h3><p>Confirm your current login password to update your name and address. This does not change your password.</p><form onSubmit={saveProfile} key={profile.name + profile.address}><Field label="Name" name="name" defaultValue={profile.name} required maxLength={200} autoComplete="name" /><Field label="Address" name="address" defaultValue={profile.address} required maxLength={500} autoComplete="street-address" /><Field label="Email · read only" value={profile.email} readOnly type="email" /><Field label="Current password for verification" name="password" type="password" required autoComplete="current-password" /><button className="button" disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Verifying…' : 'Verify and save details ↗'}</button></form></section>}
    {admin && tab === 'Statements' && accountId && <><section className="panel"><h3>Live ledger preview</h3><p>A live calculation, not an issued statement. Backdated entries change this and later months. Issue new revisions for every affected month; existing PDFs stay unchanged.</p><div className="toolbar"><Field label="Statement month" type="month" value={month} onChange={e => { setMonth(e.target.value); setPreview(null); }} required /><button className="button small" disabled={busy || !month} onClick={() => perform(async () => setPreview(await api(`${base}/preview?month=${encodeURIComponent(month)}`)))}>Calculate preview ↗</button></div>{preview && <><div className="preview-account"><strong>{preview.account.name}</strong><span>{preview.account.number} · {preview.account.address}</span></div><div className="stat-grid">{['opening', 'deposits', 'debits', 'closing'].map(k => <div key={k}><span>{k}</span><strong>{money(preview[k])}</strong></div>)}</div><Transactions rows={preview.transactions} running /><button className="button" disabled={busy || preview.month !== month} onClick={() => perform(async () => { await api(`${base}/statements`, { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ month }) }); setArchive(await api(`${base}/statements`)); }, 'Immutable statement revision issued.')}>{busy ? 'Issuing…' : `Issue ${month} revision ↗`}</button></>}</section><section className="panel"><h3>Issued statement archive</h3><p className="fine-print">Immutable PDF snapshots. Integrity checks validate the snapshot, PDF and stored archive.</p>{archive.length ? archive.map(s => <article className="archive-row" key={s.id}><div><h4>{s.month} <span className="role-badge">Revision {s.revision}</span></h4><p>{money(s.closing)} closing · Issued {new Date(s.issuedAt).toLocaleString()}</p><details><summary>Snapshot hash</summary><code>{s.hash}</code></details>{integrity[s.id] && <p className={integrity[s.id].valid ? 'positive' : 'error'} role="status">{integrity[s.id].valid ? 'Integrity verified' : 'Integrity failed — do not rely on this revision'}</p>}</div><div className="archive-actions"><button className="text-button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => pdf(s)}>Download PDF ↗</button><button className="text-button" disabled={busy} onClick={() => perform(async () => { const result = await api(`/admin/statements/${s.id}/integrity`); setIntegrity(i => ({ ...i, [s.id]: result })); })}>Check integrity</button></div></article>) : <p>No issued statements for this account.</p>}</section></>}
    {admin && tab === 'Investments' && <section className="panel"><div className="panel-heading"><h3>Restricted investment records</h3><button className="button small" disabled={busy} onClick={() => setEdit({ type: 'investment' })}>Create record +</button></div><p className="fine-print">Internal demonstration records, not investment offers or verified valuations.</p><div className="investment-grid">{investments.map(i => <article className="investment-card" key={i.id}><span className="role-badge">{i.status}</span><h3>{i.sector}</h3><h4>{money(i.targetCapital)}</h4><span className="eyebrow">TARGET CAPITAL</span><p><strong>Risk:</strong> {i.risk}</p><p>{i.strategy}</p><button className="link-arrow" onClick={() => setEdit({ type: 'investment', item: i })}>Edit record ↗</button></article>)}</div>{!investments.length && <p>No investment records. Create a record to begin.</p>}</section>}
    {admin && tab === 'Audit' && <section className="panel"><h3>Operation audit trail</h3><div className="table-scroll"><table><thead><tr><th>Time</th><th>Action</th><th>Actor</th><th>Target</th><th>Details</th></tr></thead><tbody>{audit.map(a => <tr key={a.id}><td>{a.createdAt || a.at || a.timestamp}</td><td>{a.action}</td><td>{a.actorId ?? 'Anonymous'}</td><td>{a.entity ?? a.targetId ?? a.target}</td><td><details><summary>View payload</summary><pre>{JSON.stringify(a.details, null, 2)}</pre></details></td></tr>)}</tbody></table></div>{!audit.length && <p>No audit records available.</p>}</section>}
    </div>{edit && <Modal title={edit.type === 'post' ? 'Post a ledger entry.' : edit.type === 'reverse' ? `Reverse entry #${edit.item.id}.` : edit.item ? 'Edit investment record.' : 'Create investment record.'} onClose={() => !busy && setEdit(null)}>{error && <div className="error" role="alert">{error}</div>}{edit.type === 'investment' ? <form onSubmit={saveInvestment}><Field label="Sector" name="sector" defaultValue={edit.item?.sector || ''} required maxLength={100} list="investment-sectors" /><datalist id="investment-sectors">{['Startup technologies', 'Small data centers', 'REITs', 'New home builders'].map(v => <option key={v} value={v} />)}</datalist><Field label="Status" name="status" defaultValue={edit.item?.status || 'Review'} required maxLength={100} /><Field label="Target capital · USD" name="targetCapital" type="number" step="0.01" min="0" max="1000000000" defaultValue={edit.item ? edit.item.targetCapital / 100 : ''} required /><Field label="Risk" name="risk" defaultValue={edit.item?.risk || ''} required maxLength={200} /><label>Strategy<textarea name="strategy" defaultValue={edit.item?.strategy || ''} required maxLength={2000} rows={5} /></label><button className="button" disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Saving…' : 'Save investment record ↗'}</button></form> : <form onSubmit={saveEntry}><p>{edit.type === 'reverse' ? `${edit.item.description} · ${money(edit.item.amount)}. A correction of ${money(-edit.item.amount)} will be recorded; the original remains unchanged.` : `Selected account: ${data?.account.name}. Positive amounts credit the account; negative amounts debit it.`}</p><Field label="Ledger date" name="date" type="date" defaultValue={today()} min={edit.type === 'reverse' ? edit.item.date : '1900-01-01'} required />{edit.type === 'reverse' ? <Field label="Reason for reversal" name="reason" required maxLength={250} /> : <><Field label="Description" name="description" required maxLength={300} /><Field label="Signed amount · USD" name="amount" type="number" step="0.01" required max="1000000000" min="-1000000000" /></>}<button className="button" disabled={busy}>{busy ? 'Recording…' : edit.type === 'reverse' ? 'Record reversal ↗' : 'Post transaction ↗'}</button></form>}</Modal>}</div>;
}

import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react';
import * as THREE from 'three';
import { OrbitControls } from 'three/addons/controls/OrbitControls.js';

function polygon(points) {
  const shape = new THREE.Shape();
  points.forEach(([x, y], i) => i ? shape.lineTo(x, y) : shape.moveTo(x, y));
  shape.closePath();
  return shape;
}
function model(kind) {
  const group = new THREE.Group();
  const copper = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#c48c69', metalness: 0.8, roughness: 0.28 });
  const navy = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#233d53', metalness: 0.65, roughness: 0.35 });
  const ice = new THREE.MeshStandardMaterial({ color: '#b7e9f6', emissive: '#5cbed7', emissiveIntensity: 0.7, metalness: 0.4, roughness: 0.2 });
  const box = (x, y, z, w, h, d, material = navy) => { const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.BoxGeometry(w, h, d), material); mesh.position.set(x, y, z); group.add(mesh); return mesh; };
  if (kind === 'brand') {
    const letters = [
      [[0,0],[0.9,0],[0.9,0.35],[0.3,0.35],[0.3,0.7],[0.9,0.7],[0.9,1.65],[0,1.65],[0,1.3],[0.6,1.3],[0.6,1.05],[0,1.05]],
      [[0,1.65],[0.32,1.65],[0.55,0.4],[0.8,1.65],[1.12,1.65],[0.74,0],[0.36,0]],
      [[0,0],[0.95,0],[1.1,0.22],[1.1,0.7],[0.9,0.86],[1.1,1.03],[1.1,1.43],[0.9,1.65],[0,1.65]],
      [[0,1.65],[1.12,1.65],[1.12,1.32],[0.72,1.32],[0.72,0],[0.4,0],[0.4,1.32],[0,1.32]]
    ];
    letters.forEach((points, i) => {
      const shape = polygon(points);
      if (i === 2) { shape.holes.push(polygon([[0.3,0.3],[0.75,0.3],[0.75,0.63],[0.3,0.63]]), polygon([[0.3,1],[0.75,1],[0.75,1.34],[0.3,1.34]])); }
      const mesh = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ExtrudeGeometry(shape, { depth: 0.38, bevelEnabled: true, bevelSize: 0.045, bevelThickness: 0.045, bevelSegments: 3, steps: 1 }), i === 1 ? ice : copper);
      mesh.position.set(i * 1.25 - 2.4, -0.8, 0);
      group.add(mesh);
    });
    for (let i = 0; i < 3; i++) { const ring = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.TorusGeometry(3.05 + i * 0.32, 0.012, 8, 120), i === 1 ? ice : navy); ring.rotation.set(1.05 + i * 0.2, i * 0.6, 0.2); group.add(ring); }
    group.rotation.set(0.12, -0.22, -0.08);
  } else {
    box(0, -0.8, 0, 4.8, 0.2, 3.4, copper);
    if (kind === 'data') {
      for (let x = 0; x < 4; x++) for (let z = 0; z < 2; z++) { box(x * 1.05 - 1.6, 0.1, z * 1.4 - 0.65, 0.72, 1.7, 0.85); for (let y = 0; y < 6; y++) { box(x * 1.05 - 1.6, y * 0.22 - 0.45, z * 1.4 - 0.21, 0.55, 0.05, 0.03, ice); } }
    } else if (kind === 'reits') {
      for (let i = 0; i < 5; i++) { const h = 1.2 + (i % 3) * 0.65; const x = (i % 3) * 1.45 - 1.45; const z = Math.floor(i / 3) * 1.5 - 0.65; box(x, h / 2 - 0.7, z, 0.95, h, 0.95); for (let y = 0; y < h * 4; y++) box(x, y * 0.23 - 0.5, z + 0.48, 0.75, 0.08, 0.02, ice); }
    } else if (kind === 'homes') {
      for (let i = 0; i < 4; i++) { const x = (i % 2) * 2.3 - 1.15; const z = Math.floor(i / 2) * 1.6 - 0.8; box(x, -0.2, z, 1.4, 1, 1); const roof = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.ConeGeometry(1.05, 0.7, 4), copper); roof.rotation.y = Math.PI / 4; roof.position.set(x, 0.65, z); group.add(roof); box(x, -0.2, z + 0.51, 0.3, 0.65, 0.03, ice); }
    } else {
      const core = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.72, 1), copper); core.position.y = 0.3; group.add(core);
      for (let i = 0; i < 8; i++) { const a = i * Math.PI / 4; const p = new THREE.Vector3(Math.cos(a) * 1.8, Math.sin(a * 2) * 0.5 + 0.3, Math.sin(a) * 1.4); const node = new THREE.Mesh(new THREE.IcosahedronGeometry(0.2, 0), ice); node.position.copy(p); group.add(node); const line = new THREE.Line(new THREE.BufferGeometry().setFromPoints([new THREE.Vector3(0, 0.3, 0), p]), new THREE.LineBasicMaterial({ color: '#94cdda' })); group.add(line); }
    }
    group.rotation.y = -0.35;
  }
  return group;
}

export default function Scene({ kind = 'brand', label }) {
  const host = useRef(null);
  const engine = useRef(null);
  const [paused, setPaused] = useState(() => matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)').matches);
  const [failed, setFailed] = useState(false);
  const pauseRef = useRef(paused);
  useEffect(() => { pauseRef.current = paused; engine.current?.sync(); }, [paused]);
  useEffect(() => {
    const container = host.current;
    let renderer;
    try { renderer = new THREE.WebGLRenderer({ antialias: true, alpha: true, powerPreference: 'low-power' }); } catch { setFailed(true); return; }
    renderer.setPixelRatio(Math.min(devicePixelRatio, 1.5));
    renderer.setClearColor(0x07111e, 0);
    renderer.toneMapping = THREE.ACESFilmicToneMapping;
    renderer.toneMappingExposure = 1.4;
    container.appendChild(renderer.domElement);
    const scene = new THREE.Scene();
    const object = model(kind); scene.add(object);
    const camera = new THREE.PerspectiveCamera(38, 1, 0.1, 100);
    camera.position.set(kind === 'brand' ? 0.7 : 5, kind === 'brand' ? 2.4 : 4, kind === 'brand' ? 10.8 : 7);
    const controls = new OrbitControls(camera, renderer.domElement);
    controls.enableZoom = false; controls.enablePan = false; controls.enableDamping = false; controls.target.set(0, 0, 0); controls.update();
    scene.add(new THREE.HemisphereLight(0xb8e7ff, 0x172033, 2.8));
    const key = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0xffceaa, 5); key.position.set(2, 5, 5); scene.add(key);
    const rim = new THREE.DirectionalLight(0x8edbff, 4); rim.position.set(-4, 2, -3); scene.add(rim);
    let visible = false, frame = 0, last = 0, phase = 0, dirty = true;
    function draw() { if (visible && !document.hidden) renderer.render(scene, camera); }
    function tick(time) { frame = 0; if (!visible || document.hidden || pauseRef.current) return; if (time - last >= 1000 / 30) { phase += 0.006; object.rotation.y += 0.002; key.position.x = Math.cos(phase) * 5; rim.intensity = 3.5 + Math.sin(phase) * 0.7; draw(); last = time; } frame = requestAnimationFrame(tick); }
    function sync() { cancelAnimationFrame(frame); frame = 0; if (visible && !document.hidden) { if (dirty || pauseRef.current) { draw(); dirty = false; } if (!pauseRef.current) frame = requestAnimationFrame(tick); } }
    engine.current = { sync, orbit: delta => { const offset = camera.position.clone().sub(controls.target); offset.applyAxisAngle(new THREE.Vector3(0, 1, 0), delta); camera.position.copy(offset.add(controls.target)); controls.update(); draw(); } };
    const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => { visible = entries[0].isIntersecting; dirty = true; sync(); }); observer.observe(container);
    const resize = new ResizeObserver(() => { const w = container.clientWidth, h = container.clientHeight; if (!w || !h) return; renderer.setSize(w, h); camera.aspect = w / h; camera.updateProjectionMatrix(); dirty = true; sync(); }); resize.observe(container);
    const media = matchMedia('(prefers-reduced-motion: reduce)'); const change = () => { setPaused(media.matches); }; media.addEventListener('change', change);
    controls.addEventListener('change', draw); document.addEventListener('visibilitychange', sync);
    const loss = e => { e.preventDefault(); pauseRef.current = true; setPaused(true); setFailed(true); cancelAnimationFrame(frame); }; renderer.domElement.addEventListener('webglcontextlost', loss);
    return () => { engine.current = null; cancelAnimationFrame(frame); observer.disconnect(); resize.disconnect(); media.removeEventListener('change', change); document.removeEventListener('visibilitychange', sync); controls.dispose(); scene.traverse(o => { o.geometry?.dispose(); if (o.material) (Array.isArray(o.material) ? o.material : [o.material]).forEach(m => m.dispose()); }); renderer.dispose(); renderer.domElement.remove(); };
  }, [kind]);
  return <><div ref={host} className="scene-canvas" role="img" aria-label={label} />{failed && <div className="scene-fallback">{kind === 'brand' ? 'SVBT' : kind === 'data' ? 'INFRASTRUCTURE' : kind === 'homes' ? 'BUILD' : kind === 'reits' ? 'REAL ASSETS' : 'SEED'}<span>Spatial view unavailable · {label}</span></div>}<div className="scene-controls">{!failed && <><button aria-label="Orbit model left" onClick={() => engine.current?.orbit(-0.25)}>←</button><button aria-label="Orbit model right" onClick={() => engine.current?.orbit(0.25)}>→</button><button aria-pressed={paused} onClick={() => setPaused(!paused)}>{paused ? 'Play motion' : 'Pause motion'}</button><span>DRAG TO ORBIT</span></>}</div></>;
}

solid var(--border);padding:7px 16px}.status-dot{height:5px;width:5px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--copper);box-shadow:0 0 9px ================================================================================
FILE: server/create-moderator.js
================================================================================
import 'dotenv/config';
import { resolve } from 'node:path';
import { openDatabase, hashPassword, atomic, audit, demoCredentials } from './db.js';
import { text } from './services.js';

const email = text(process.env.MODERATOR_EMAIL, 'MODERATOR_EMAIL', 254).toLowerCase();
const password = process.env.MODERATOR_PASSWORD;
if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(email)) throw new Error('MODERATOR_EMAIL must be a valid email address.');
if (typeof password !== 'string' || password.length < 12 || password.length > 1024) throw new Error('MODERATOR_PASSWORD must contain 12–1024 characters.');
if (Object.values(demoCredentials).some(([, secret]) => secret === password)) throw new Error('Provisioning requires a unique password, not a demonstration password.');
const name = text(process.env.MODERATOR_NAME || 'Timothy Powers', 'MODERATOR_NAME', 200);
const address = text(process.env.MODERATOR_ADDRESS || 'Silicon Valley, California', 'MODERATOR_ADDRESS', 500);
const passwordHash = await hashPassword(password);
const db = openDatabase(resolve(process.env.DB_PATH || 'data/bank.sqlite'));
try {
  const id = atomic(db, () => {
    const existing = db.prepare('SELECT id,role FROM users WHERE email=?').get(email);
    if (existing && process.env.MODERATOR_RESET === 'true') {
      if (existing.role !== 'moderator') throw new Error('This script never escalates customer accounts.');
      db.prepare('UPDATE users SET passwordHash=? WHERE id=?').run(passwordHash, existing.id);
      db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE userId=?').run(existing.id);
      audit(db, existing.id, 'moderator.password-reset', existing.id, { email });
      return existing.id;
    }
    if (existing) throw new Error('That email already exists. This script never escalates or overwrites existing users.');
    const userId = Number(db.prepare('INSERT INTO users(email,name,address,role,passwordHash) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)').run(email, name, address, 'moderator', passwordHash).lastInsertRowid);
    audit(db, userId, 'moderator.provision', userId, { email });
    return userId;
  });
  console.log(`Moderator ${email} provisioned as user ${id}.`);
} finally { db.close(); }


================================================================================
FILE: server/db.js
================================================================================
import { DatabaseSync } from 'node:sqlite';
import { randomBytes, randomInt, scrypt, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
import { promisify } from 'node:util';
import { mkdirSync } from 'node:fs';
import { dirname } from 'node:path';

const derive = promisify(scrypt);
export const demoCredentials = { customer: [process.env.DEMO_CUSTOMER_EMAIL || 'sales@bakhtiariautogroup.com', process.env.DEMO_CUSTOMER_PASSWORD || 'FounderClient!2026'], moderator: [process.env.DEMO_MODERATOR_EMAIL || 'tpower@siliconvalleybankandtrust.com', process.env.DEMO_MODERATOR_PASSWORD || 'ModeratorGod!2026'] };
export async function hashPassword(password) {
  const salt = randomBytes(32).toString('hex');
  const hash = await derive(password, salt, 64, { N: 131072, r: 8, p: 1, maxmem: 256 * 1024 * 1024 });
  return `scrypt$131072$8$1$${salt}$${hash.toString('hex')}`;
}
export async function verifyPassword(password, encoded) {
  const [algorithm, n, r, p, salt, hex] = encoded.split('$');
  if (algorithm !== 'scrypt' || n !== '131072' || r !== '8' || p !== '1' || !/^[a-f0-9]{128}$/.test(hex || '')) return false;
  const actual = await derive(password, salt, 64, { N: 131072, r: 8, p: 1, maxmem: 256 * 1024 * 1024 });
  return timingSafeEqual(actual, Buffer.from(hex, 'hex'));
}
export function atomic(db, operation) {
  db.exec('BEGIN IMMEDIATE');
  try { const result = operation(); db.exec('COMMIT'); return result; } catch (error) { db.exec('ROLLBACK'); throw error; }
}
export function audit(db, actorId, action, entity, details = {}) {
  db.prepare('INSERT INTO audit(actorId, action, entity, details) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)').run(actorId ?? null, action, String(entity), JSON.stringify(details));
}
export function newAccount(db, userId, name, address) {
  let number;
  do { number = String(randomInt(100000, 1000000)) + String(randomInt(0, 1000000)).padStart(6, '0'); } while (db.prepare('SELECT id FROM accounts WHERE number = ?').get(number));
  return Number(db.prepare('INSERT INTO accounts(userId, number, name, address) VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)').run(userId, number, name, address).lastInsertRowid);
}
export function openDatabase(path) {
  if (path !== ':memory:') mkdirSync(dirname(path), { recursive: true });
  const db = new DatabaseSync(path);
  db.exec(`PRAGMA journal_mode=WAL; PRAGMA foreign_keys=ON; PRAGMA busy_timeout=5000;
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS users(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, email TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE COLLATE NOCASE, name TEXT NOT NULL, address TEXT NOT NULL, role TEXT NOT NULL CHECK(role IN ('customer','moderator')), passwordHash TEXT NOT NULL);
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS accounts(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, userId INTEGER NOT NULL UNIQUE REFERENCES users(id), number TEXT NOT NULL UNIQUE CHECK(length(number)=12 AND number NOT GLOB '*[^0-9]*'), name TEXT NOT NULL, address TEXT NOT NULL);
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS transactions(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, accountId INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES accounts(id), date TEXT NOT NULL, description TEXT NOT NULL, amount INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(typeof(amount)='integer' AND amount != 0 AND abs(amount)<=100000000000), reversalOf INTEGER UNIQUE REFERENCES transactions(id), actorId INTEGER REFERENCES users(id), createdAt TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ','now')));
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS sessions(tokenHash TEXT PRIMARY KEY, userId INTEGER REFERENCES users(id), csrfToken TEXT NOT NULL, expiresAt INTEGER NOT NULL);
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS statements(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, accountId INTEGER NOT NULL REFERENCES accounts(id), month TEXT NOT NULL, revision INTEGER NOT NULL, issuedAt TEXT NOT NULL, closing INTEGER NOT NULL, snapshot TEXT NOT NULL, hash TEXT NOT NULL, pdf BLOB NOT NULL, pdfHash TEXT NOT NULL, archivePath TEXT NOT NULL, UNIQUE(accountId,month,revision));
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS investments(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, sector TEXT NOT NULL, status TEXT NOT NULL, targetCapital INTEGER NOT NULL CHECK(typeof(targetCapital)='integer' AND targetCapital>=0 AND targetCapital<=100000000000), risk TEXT NOT NULL, strategy TEXT NOT NULL);
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS audit(id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY, actorId INTEGER REFERENCES users(id), action TEXT NOT NULL, entity TEXT NOT NULL, details TEXT NOT NULL, createdAt TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT (strftime('%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%fZ','now')));
    CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS metadata(key TEXT PRIMARY KEY, value TEXT NOT NULL);
    CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS valid_reversal BEFORE INSERT ON transactions WHEN NEW.reversalOf IS NOT NULL BEGIN
      SELECT CASE WHEN NOT EXISTS(SELECT 1 FROM transactions WHERE id=NEW.reversalOf AND accountId=NEW.accountId AND amount=-NEW.amount AND reversalOf IS NULL AND date<=NEW.date) THEN RAISE(ABORT,'Invalid reversal') END;
    END;
  `);
  for (const table of ['transactions', 'statements', 'audit']) {
    for (const operation of ['UPDATE', 'DELETE']) db.exec(`CREATE TRIGGER IF NOT EXISTS immutable_${table}_${operation.toLowerCase()} BEFORE ${operation} ON ${table} BEGIN SELECT RAISE(ABORT,'Immutable ${table}'); END;`);
  }
  return db;
}
export async function seedDemo(db) {
  if (db.prepare("SELECT value FROM metadata WHERE key='demo-seeded'").get()) return;
  const customerHash = await hashPassword(demoCredentials.customer[1]);
  const moderatorHash = await hashPassword(demoCredentials.moderator[1]);
  atomic(db, () => {
    if (db.prepare("SELECT value FROM metadata WHERE key='demo-seeded'").get()) return;
    const customer = Number(db.prepare('INSERT INTO users(email,name,address,role,passwordHash) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)').run(demoCredentials.customer[0], 'Bakhtiari Auto Group, LLC', '3155 Stevens Creek, San Jose, CA 95117', 'customer', customerHash).lastInsertRowid);
    const moderator = Number(db.prepare('INSERT INTO users(email,name,address,role,passwordHash) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)').run(demoCredentials.moderator[0], 'Timothy Powers', 'Silicon Valley, California', 'moderator', moderatorHash).lastInsertRowid);
    const accountId = newAccount(db, customer, 'Bakhtiari Auto Group, LLC', '3155 Stevens Creek, San Jose, CA 95117');
    const descriptions = ['Cloud infrastructure', 'Software subscriptions', 'Office rent', 'Internet and communications', 'Business insurance', 'Accounting services', 'Digital marketing', 'Contract engineering', 'Office supplies', 'Security monitoring', 'Technology hardware', 'Business travel'];
    const base = [65000, 32000, 180000, 18000, 28000, 45000, 50000, 60000, 12000, 22000, 35000, 29700];
    const insert = db.prepare('INSERT INTO transactions(accountId,date,description,amount,actorId) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)');
    for (let m = 0; m < 4; m++) {
      const month = `2026-${String(m + 6).padStart(2, '0')}`;
      insert.run(accountId, `${month}-02`, m ? 'Investment funding from Bakhtiari Corp. Wells Fargo Account' : 'Opening deposit', m ? 1500000 : 1000000, moderator);
      descriptions.forEach((description, i) => insert.run(accountId, `${month}-${String(i + 3).padStart(2, '0')}`, description, -(base[i] + (i === 11 ? m * 11700 : 0)), moderator));
    }
    for (const [sector, capital, strategy] of [['Startup technologies', 250000000, 'Milestone-based capital and runway planning'], ['Small data centers', 500000000, 'Capacity, cooling and energy resilience'], ['REITs', 350000000, 'Diversified property cash-flow review']]) db.prepare('INSERT INTO investments(sector,status,targetCapital,risk,strategy) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)').run(sector, 'Review', capital, 'Capital at risk', strategy);
    audit(db, moderator, 'demo.seed', accountId, { months: 4 });
    db.prepare("INSERT INTO metadata(key,value) VALUES ('demo-seeded',?)").run(String(accountId));
  });
}

import 'dotenv/config';
import express from 'express';
import { randomBytes, timingSafeEqual } from 'node:crypto';
import { resolve, dirname } from 'node:path';
import { fileURLToPath, pathToFileURL } from 'node:url';
import { openDatabase, seedDemo, demoCredentials, hashPassword, verifyPassword, atomic, audit, newAccount } from './db.js';
import { digest, fail, text, accountData, postTransaction, reverseTransaction, preview, archive, statement, integrity, issueStatement, saveInvestment } from './services.js';

const root = resolve(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..');
let authenticationActive = 0;
async function authenticate(operation, res) {
  if (authenticationActive >= 2) {
    res.set('Retry-After', '1');
    fail(429, 'Authentication capacity is busy. Try again later.');
  }
  authenticationActive++;
  try { return await operation(); } finally { authenticationActive--; }
}
export function configuration(options = {}) {
  const production = options.production ?? process.env.NODE_ENV === 'production';
  const origin = options.origin ?? process.env.APP_ORIGIN ?? (production ? '' : 'http://localhost:3000');
  let parsed;
  try { parsed = new URL(origin); } catch { throw new Error('APP_ORIGIN must be an explicit valid origin.'); }
  if (parsed.origin !== origin || !['http:', 'https:'].includes(parsed.protocol) || (production && parsed.protocol !== 'https:')) throw new Error('APP_ORIGIN must be an exact origin, HTTPS in production.');
  const demoSeed = options.demoSeed ?? (process.env.DEMO_SEED === undefined ? !production : process.env.DEMO_SEED === 'true');
  if (production && demoSeed) throw new Error('Demonstration seeding is forbidden in production.');
  const authRateLimit = options.authRateLimit ?? 30;
  if (!Number.isSafeInteger(authRateLimit) || authRateLimit < 1) throw new Error('authRateLimit must be a positive integer.');
  return { production, origin, demoSeed, authRateLimit, frontend: options.frontend ?? true, dbPath: options.dbPath ?? resolve(process.env.DB_PATH || resolve(root, 'data/bank.sqlite')), statementDir: resolve(options.statementDir ?? process.env.STATEMENT_DIR ?? resolve(root, 'monthly/statements')), trustProxy: options.trustProxy ?? process.env.TRUST_PROXY === 'true', dev: options.dev ?? false };
}
function publicUser(user) { return user ? { id: user.id, name: user.name, email: user.email, role: user.role } : null; }
function email(value) {
  const normalized = text(value, 'Email', 254).toLowerCase();
  if (!/^[^\s@]+@[^\s@]+\.[^\s@]+$/.test(normalized)) fail(400, 'A valid email address is required.');
  return normalized;
}
function password(value, registration = false) {
  if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.length > 1024 || value.length < (registration ? 12 : 1)) fail(400, registration ? 'Password must contain 12–1024 characters.' : 'Password is required.');
  return value;
}
function identifier(value) { if (!/^[1-9]\d*$/.test(value) || !Number.isSafeInteger(Number(value))) fail(400, 'Invalid record identifier.'); return Number(value); }
export async function createApp(options = {}) {
  const config = configuration(options), db = openDatabase(config.dbPath);
  let vite;
  try {
    if (config.production) {
      if (db.prepare("SELECT value FROM metadata WHERE key='demo-seeded'").get()) throw new Error('Production cannot use a demo-seeded database. Use a fresh database for handover.');
      const demoUsers = db.prepare('SELECT passwordHash FROM users WHERE email IN (?,?)').all(demoCredentials.customer[0], demoCredentials.moderator[0]);
      for (const user of demoUsers) for (const [, secret] of Object.values(demoCredentials)) if (await verifyPassword(secret, user.passwordHash)) throw new Error('Production database contains demonstration credentials. Use a fresh database and provision a unique moderator.');
    }
    if (config.demoSeed) {
      await seedDemo(db);
      const seedAccount = Number(db.prepare("SELECT value FROM metadata WHERE key='demo-seeded'").get().value);
      const moderator = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE email=?').get(demoCredentials.moderator[0]);
      for (const month of ['2026-06', '2026-07', '2026-08', '2026-09']) if (!db.prepare('SELECT id FROM statements WHERE accountId=? AND month=?').get(seedAccount, month)) await issueStatement(db, seedAccount, month, moderator.id, config.statementDir);
    }
    const dummyHash = await hashPassword(randomBytes(32).toString('hex'));
    const app = express();
    app.disable('x-powered-by');
    if (config.trustProxy) app.set('trust proxy', 1);
    app.use((req, res, next) => {
      res.set({ 'X-Content-Type-Options': 'nosniff', 'Referrer-Policy': 'same-origin', 'X-Frame-Options': 'DENY' });
      if (config.production) res.set({ 'Strict-Transport-Security': 'max-age=31536000', 'Content-Security-Policy': "default-src 'self'; script-src 'self'; style-src 'self' 'unsafe-inline'; img-src 'self' data: blob:; font-src 'self'; connect-src 'self'; worker-src 'self' blob:; object-src 'none'; base-uri 'none'; form-action 'self'; frame-ancestors 'none'" });
      next();
    });
    app.use('/api', (req, res, next) => {
      res.set('Cache-Control', 'no-store');
      if (req.get('origin') && req.get('origin') !== config.origin) return next(Object.assign(new Error('Request origin is not allowed.'), { status: 403 }));
      if (!['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS'].includes(req.method) && req.get('origin') !== config.origin) return next(Object.assign(new Error('An exact matching Origin is required for write requests.'), { status: 403 }));
      if (req.get('sec-fetch-site') === 'cross-site') return next(Object.assign(new Error('Cross-site requests are forbidden.'), { status: 403 }));
      next();
    });
    app.get('/api/health', (req, res) => res.json({ status: 'ok' }));
    app.use('/api', express.json({ limit: '16kb', strict: true }));
    const cookieName = 'svbt_session';
    function makeSession(req, res, userId) {
      const token = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url'), csrfToken = randomBytes(32).toString('base64url');
      atomic(db, () => {
        if (req.session) db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE tokenHash=?').run(req.session.tokenHash);
        db.prepare('DELETE FROM sessions WHERE expiresAt<=?').run(Date.now());
        db.prepare('INSERT INTO sessions(tokenHash,userId,csrfToken,expiresAt) VALUES (?,?,?,?)').run(digest(token), userId ?? null, csrfToken, Date.now() + 8 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
      });
      res.cookie(cookieName, token, { httpOnly: true, secure: true, sameSite: 'strict', path: '/', maxAge: 8 * 60 * 60 * 1000 });
      req.session = { tokenHash: digest(token), userId, csrfToken };
      return csrfToken;
    }
    app.use('/api', (req, res, next) => {
      const tokens = (req.get('cookie') || '').split(';').map(item => item.trim()).filter(item => item.startsWith(`${cookieName}=`));
      const token = tokens.length === 1 ? tokens[0].slice(cookieName.length + 1) : '';
      if (/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$/.test(token)) req.session = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM sessions WHERE tokenHash=? AND expiresAt>?').get(digest(token), Date.now());
      req.user = req.session?.userId ? db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=?').get(req.session.userId) : null;
      if (!['GET', 'HEAD', 'OPTIONS'].includes(req.method)) {
        const provided = req.get('x-csrf-token') || '', expected = req.session?.csrfToken || '';
        if (!expected || Buffer.byteLength(provided) !== Buffer.byteLength(expected) || !timingSafeEqual(Buffer.from(provided), Buffer.from(expected))) return next(Object.assign(new Error('A valid session CSRF token is required.'), { status: 403 }));
        if (!req.is('application/json')) return next(Object.assign(new Error('Write requests require application/json.'), { status: 415 }));
        if (!req.body || typeof req.body !== 'object' || Array.isArray(req.body)) return next(Object.assign(new Error('A JSON object is required.'), { status: 400 }));
      }
      next();
    });
    const auth = (req, res, next) => req.user ? next() : next(Object.assign(new Error('Sign in to access this resource.'), { status: 401 }));
    const moderator = (req, res, next) => !req.user ? next(Object.assign(new Error('Sign in to access operations.'), { status: 401 })) : req.user.role === 'moderator' ? next() : next(Object.assign(new Error('Moderator permission is required.'), { status: 403 }));
    app.get('/api/session', (req, res) => res.json({ user: publicUser(req.user), csrfToken: req.session?.csrfToken || makeSession(req, res, null) }));
    const attempts = new Map();
    const limiter = (req, res, next) => {
      const key = req.ip, now = Date.now();
      for (const [ip, value] of attempts) if (value.until <= now) attempts.delete(ip);
      const current = attempts.get(key) || { count: 0, until: now + 15 * 60 * 1000 };
      if ((!attempts.has(key) && attempts.size >= 10000) || current.count >= config.authRateLimit) { res.set('Retry-After', String(Math.max(1, Math.ceil((current.until - now) / 1000)))); return next(Object.assign(new Error('Too many authentication attempts. Try again later.'), { status: 429 })); }
      current.count++;
      attempts.set(key, current); next();
    };
    app.post('/api/register', limiter, async (req, res) => {
      const values = req.body, userEmail = email(values.email), name = text(values.name, 'Name', 200), address = text(values.address, 'Address', 500);
      if (Object.hasOwn(values, 'role') && values.role !== 'customer') fail(400, 'Registration can only create customers.');
      if (db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE email=?').get(userEmail)) fail(409, 'This email address is already registered.');
      const secret = password(values.password, true);
      const passwordHash = await authenticate(() => hashPassword(secret), res);
      const id = atomic(db, () => {
        if (db.prepare('SELECT id FROM users WHERE email=?').get(userEmail)) fail(409, 'This email address is already registered.');
        const userId = Number(db.prepare('INSERT INTO users(email,name,address,role,passwordHash) VALUES (?,?,?,?,?)').run(userEmail, name, address, 'customer', passwordHash).lastInsertRowid);
        const accountId = newAccount(db, userId, name, address);
        audit(db, userId, 'user.register', accountId);
        return userId;
      });
      res.status(201).json({ user: publicUser(db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE id=?').get(id)), csrfToken: makeSession(req, res, id) });
    });
    app.post('/api/login', limiter, async (req, res) => {
      const userEmail = email(req.body.email), secret = password(req.body.password);
      const user = db.prepare('SELECT * FROM users WHERE email=?').get(userEmail);
      const verified = await authenticate(() => verifyPassword(secret, user?.passwordHash || dummyHash), res);
      if (!user || !verified) fail(401, 'Email or password is incorrect.');
      audit(db, user.id, 'session.login', user.id);
      res.json({ user: publicUser(user), csrfToken: makeSession(req, res, user.id) });
    });
    app.post('/api/logout', (req, res) => {
      if (req.user) audit(db, req.user.id, 'session.logout', req.user.id);
      res.json({ user: null, csrfToken: makeSession(req, res, null) });
    });
    app.get('/api/profile', auth, (req, res) => res.json({ ...publicUser(req.user), address: req.user.address }));
    app.patch('/api/profile', auth, limiter, async (req, res) => {
      const name = text(req.body.name, 'Name', 200), address = text(req.body.address, 'Address', 500);
      const secret = password(req.body.password);
      if (!await authenticate(() => verifyPassword(secret, req.user.passwordHash), res)) fail(401, 'Current password verification failed.');
      atomic(db, () => {
        db.prepare('UPDATE users SET name=?,address=? WHERE id=?').run(name, address, req.user.id);
        db.prepare('UPDATE accounts SET name=?,address=? WHERE userId=?').run(name, address, req.user.id);
        audit(db, req.user.id, 'profile.update', req.user.id, { name, address });
      });
      res.json({ ...publicUser(req.user), name, address });
    });
    app.get('/api/account', auth, (req, res) => {
      const row = db.prepare('SELECT id FROM accounts WHERE userId=?').get(req.user.id);
      if (!row) fail(404, 'No customer account is associated with this user.');
      res.json(accountData(db, row.id));
    });
    app.use('/api/admin', moderator);
    app.get('/api/admin/accounts', (req, res) => res.json(db.prepare('SELECT * FROM accounts ORDER BY id').all()));
    app.get('/api/admin/accounts/:id', (req, res) => res.json(accountData(db, identifier(req.params.id))));
    app.post('/api/admin/accounts/:id/transactions', (req, res) => res.status(201).json(postTransaction(db, identifier(req.params.id), req.body, req.user.id)));
    app.post('/api/admin/transactions/:id/reverse', (req, res) => res.status(201).json(reverseTransaction(db, identifier(req.params.id), req.body, req.user.id)));
    app.get('/api/admin/accounts/:id/preview', (req, res) => res.json(preview(db, identifier(req.params.id), req.query.month)));
    app.get('/api/admin/accounts/:id/statements', (req, res) => res.json(archive(db, identifier(req.params.id))));
    app.post('/api/admin/accounts/:id/statements', async (req, res) => res.status(201).json(await issueStatement(db, identifier(req.params.id), req.body.month, req.user.id, config.statementDir)));
    app.get('/api/admin/statements/:id/integrity', (req, res) => res.json(integrity(db, identifier(req.params.id))));
    app.get('/api/admin/statements/:id/pdf', (req, res) => {
      const id = identifier(req.params.id), row = statement(db, id);
      if (!integrity(db, id).valid) fail(409, 'Issued statement integrity failed. PDF download is blocked.');
      res.set({ 'Content-Type': 'application/pdf', 'Content-Disposition': `attachment; filename="business-checking-${row.month}-r${row.revision}.pdf"` }).send(Buffer.from(row.pdf));
    });
    app.get('/api/admin/investments', (req, res) => res.json(db.prepare('SELECT * FROM investments ORDER BY id').all()));
    app.post('/api/admin/investments', (req, res) => res.status(201).json(saveInvestment(db, null, req.body, req.user.id)));
    app.put('/api/admin/investments/:id', (req, res) => res.json(saveInvestment(db, identifier(req.params.id), req.body, req.user.id)));
    app.get('/api/admin/audit', (req, res) => res.json(db.prepare('SELECT * FROM audit ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1000').all().map(row => ({ ...row, details: JSON.parse(row.details) }))));
    app.use('/api', (req, res) => res.status(404).json({ error: 'API endpoint not found.' }));
    if (config.frontend && config.dev) {
      const { createServer } = await import('vite');
      vite = await createServer({ root, server: { middlewareMode: true }, appType: 'spa' });
      app.use(vite.middlewares);
    } else if (config.frontend) {
      app.use(express.static(resolve(root, 'dist'), { index: false }));
      app.get('/{*path}', (req, res, next) => res.sendFile(resolve(root, 'dist/index.html'), error => error && next(error)));
    }
    app.use((error, req, res, next) => {
      if (res.headersSent) return next(error);
      const status = error.status || (error.type === 'entity.too.large' ? 413 : 500);
      if (status >= 500) console.error(error);
      res.status(status).json({ error: status >= 500 ? 'The operation could not be completed. Please try again.' : error.type === 'entity.parse.failed' ? 'Malformed JSON request.' : error.message });
    });
    const close = async () => { if (vite) await vite.close(); db.close(); };
    app.locals.db = db; app.locals.config = config; app.locals.close = close;
    return app;
  } catch (error) { if (vite) await vite.close(); db.close(); throw error; }
}
if (process.argv[1] && import.meta.url === pathToFileURL(resolve(process.argv[1])).href) {
  const app = await createApp({ dev: process.argv.includes('--dev') });
  const server = app.listen(Number(process.env.PORT || 3000), process.env.HOST || '127.0.0.1', () => console.log(`SVBT listening at ${app.locals.config.origin}`));
  for (const signal of ['SIGINT', 'SIGTERM']) process.once(signal, () => server.close(async () => { await app.locals.close(); process.exit(0); }));
}

import PDFDocument from 'pdfkit';

const money = value => new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', { style: 'currency', currency: 'USD' }).format(value / 100);
export function renderStatement(snapshot) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const document = new PDFDocument({ size: 'LETTER', margin: 48, bufferPages: true, info: { Title: `Business checking ${snapshot.month} revision ${snapshot.revision}`, Author: 'Silicon Valley Bank and Trust' } });
    const chunks = [];
    document.on('data', chunk => chunks.push(chunk));
    document.on('error', reject);
    document.on('end', () => resolve(Buffer.concat(chunks)));
    document.rect(48, 40, 68, 38).fill('#152a3b');
    document.fillColor('white').font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(19).text('SVBT', 54, 50);
    document.fillColor('#152a3b').fontSize(16).text('Silicon Valley Bank and Trust', 130, 45);
    document.fontSize(10).text('Independent demonstration · No real funds', 130, 65);
    document.fontSize(21).text('BUSINESS CHECKING STATEMENT', 48, 105);
    document.font('Helvetica').fontSize(10).text(`${snapshot.startDate} through ${snapshot.endDate} | USD | Revision ${snapshot.revision}`, 48, 137);
    document.text(`Issued ${snapshot.issuedAt}`);
    document.moveDown().font('Helvetica-Bold').text(snapshot.account.name);
    document.font('Helvetica').text(snapshot.account.address).text(`Account number: ${snapshot.account.number}`);
    document.moveDown().text(`Opening balance: ${money(snapshot.opening)}`).text(`Deposits: ${money(snapshot.deposits)}    Debits: ${money(snapshot.debits)}`).font('Helvetica-Bold').text(`Closing balance: ${money(snapshot.closing)}`).moveDown();
    const headerY = document.y;
    document.fontSize(9).text('DATE / ENTRY', 48, headerY, { width: 82 });
    document.text('DESCRIPTION', 136, headerY, { width: 235 });
    document.text('AMOUNT', 375, headerY, { width: 80, align: 'right' });
    document.text('RUNNING BALANCE', 465, headerY, { width: 99, align: 'right' });
    document.y = headerY + 20;
    for (const transaction of snapshot.transactions) {
      const description = `${transaction.description}${transaction.reversalOf ? ` (reverses #${transaction.reversalOf})` : ''}`;
      const height = Math.max(32, document.heightOfString(description, { width: 235 }) + 16);
      if (document.y + height > 710) { document.addPage(); document.font('Helvetica-Bold').fontSize(14).text('BUSINESS CHECKING · CONTINUED').font('Helvetica').fontSize(9).moveDown(); }
      const y = document.y;
      document.font('Helvetica').text(`${transaction.date}\n#${transaction.id}`, 48, y, { width: 82 });
      document.text(description, 136, y, { width: 235 });
      document.text(money(transaction.amount), 375, y, { width: 80, align: 'right' });
      document.text(money(transaction.runningBalance), 465, y, { width: 99, align: 'right' });
      document.y = y + height;
    }
    const pages = document.bufferedPageRange();
    for (let i = 0; i < pages.count; i++) {
      document.switchToPage(i);
      document.page.margins.bottom = 0;
      document.font('Helvetica').fontSize(8).fillColor('#465466').text(`Immutable issued snapshot | ${snapshot.month} | Revision ${snapshot.revision} | Page ${i + 1} of ${pages.count}`, 48, 741, { width: 516, align: 'center', lineBreak: false });
    }
    document.end();
  });
}
